=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
An election is being held with N candidates numbered 1, 2, \ldots, N. There are K votes, some of which have been counted so far.
Up until now, candidate i has received A_i votes.
After all ballots are counted, candidate i (1 \leq i \leq N) will be elected if and only if the number of candidates who have received more votes than them is less than M.  There may be multiple candidates elected.
For each candidate, find the minimum number of additional votes they need from the remaining ballots to guarantee their victory regardless of how the other candidates receive votes.
Formally, solve the following problem for each i = 1,2,\ldots,N.
Determine if there is a non-negative integer X not exceeding K - \displaystyle{\sum_{i=1}^{N}} A_i satisfying the following condition.  If it exists, find the minimum possible such integer.

- If candidate i receives X additional votes, then candidate i will always be elected.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N M K
A_1 A_2 \ldots A_N

Output

Let C_i be the minimum number of additional votes candidate i needs from the remaining ballots to guarantee their victory regardless of how other candidates receive votes. Print C_1, C_2, \ldots, C_N separated by spaces.
If candidate i has already secured their victory, then let C_i = 0. If candidate i cannot secure their victory under any circumstances, then let C_i = -1.

Constraints


- 1 \leq M \leq N \leq 2 \times 10^5
- 1 \leq K \leq 10^{12}
- 0 \leq A_i \leq 10^{12}
- \displaystyle{\sum_{i=1}^{N} A_i} \leq K
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

5 2 16
3 1 4 1 5

Sample Output 1

2 -1 1 -1 0

14 votes have been counted so far, and 2 votes are left.
The C to output is (2, -1, 1, -1, 0).  For example:

- Candidate 1 can secure their victory by obtaining 2 more votes, while not by obtaining 1 more vote.  Thus, C_1 = 2.
- Candidate 2 can never (even if they obtain 2 more votes) secure their victory, so C_2 = -1.

Sample Input 2

12 1 570
81 62 17 5 5 86 15 7 79 26 6 28

Sample Output 2

79 89 111 117 117 74 112 116 80 107 117 106

```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Let's formalize. Let current votes a_i. Candidate i final votes = a_i + x, x assigned to i. Other candidates j receive y_j >=0, sum y_j = R - x. Candidate i is elected iff count {j != i: a_j + y_j > a_i + x} <= M-1. (strictly greater). Need for all possible y_j.

To guarantee, worst-case for i is other candidates maximize number exceeding threshold T = a_i + x, given total remaining votes for others S = R - x. We need maximum possible number of other candidates with final > T. If max <= M-1 then guaranteed. If max >= M then not guaranteed (there exists distribution causing fail). Since integer votes.

We need binary search. To optimize, define local variables in loop: b_list=b, pref_list=pref, asc_list=asc, M_val=M, R_val=R, N_val=N, bisect_right local.

Function ok(x, a, r):
    T = a + x
    q = N - bisect_right(asc, T)
    if r >= M:
        if q < M:
            cnt = M - q
            s = pref[M] - pref[q]
        else:
            cnt = 0
            s = 0
    else:
        if q < M + 1:
            cnt_base = M + 1 - q
            s_base = pref[M+1] - pref[q]
        else:
            cnt_base = 0
            s_base = 0
        if r >= q:
            cnt = cnt_base - 1
            s = s_base - b[r]
        else:
            cnt = cnt_base
            s = s_base
    H = cnt * (T + 1) - s
    return H > R - x  # or H + x > R
Need ensure if q maybe > N? q=N - bisect_right(asc,T). bisect_right returns 0..N, so q 0..N. Good. If T less than all asc, bisect_right=0, q=N. If T >= all, bisect_right=N, q=0. Good.

        if ok(mid):
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid + 1
    ans = lo

Let's think about possible off-by-one in q and H.

Detailed derivation of H:
For selected positions P (indices in descending b), H = sum_{p in P} max(0, T - b[p] + 1).
Let q = first index in b with b[q] <= T. Since b descending, for p < q, b[p] > T, contribution 0. For p >= q, b[p] <= T, contribution T+1 - b[p].
Thus H = (# selected p >= q)*(T+1) - sum_{selected p>=q} b[p].
Our q = N - bisect_right(asc, T). Let's verify with duplicates equal T. b descending, q first <= T. asc = b reversed. bisect_right(asc,T) returns number of elements <= T? In ascending, bisect_right returns insertion point after any existing T, i.e., count of elements <= T. Since asc has all values, count <=T = c. Then N-c = count >T. In descending, first q is count of elements >T. So q = N - bisect_right(asc,T). Good. If T equal some b, elements equal T are at positions >=q and contribute T+1-b=1. Good.

Let selected P_i sorted by b ascending? Let's define selected values c_1 <= c_2 <= ... <= c_M (ascending). Active for threshold T=a_i+x are those c_l <= T, i.e., first k values. Then H = sum_{l=1..k} (T - c_l +1) = k(T+1) - sum_{l=1..k} c_l.
F = x + H = x + k(a_i+x+1) - sumC_k = (k+1)x + k(a_i+1) - sumC_k.
Condition F > R.

For a given k, this inequality gives x > (R - k(a_i+1) + sumC_k)/(k+1). Also x must be in interval such that exactly k selected values are <= a_i+x:
c_k <= a_i+x < c_{k+1} (with c_0=-inf, c_{M+1}=inf). Since if a_i+x >= c_k, candidate k active; if < c_{k+1}, candidate k+1 not active. Note active if c <= T, so k = count c <= a_i+x. Let's design optimized loop:

from bisect import bisect_right

def solve():
    import sys
    input = sys.stdin.readline
    N,M,K = map(int,input().split())
    A = list(map(int,input().split()))
    R = K - sum(A)
    if M == N:
        print(' '.join(['0']*N))
        return
    pairs = sorted([(A[i], i) for i in range(N)], reverse=True)
    b = [0]*N
    rank = [0]*N
    for pos, (val, idx) in enumerate(pairs):
        b[pos] = val
        rank[idx] = pos
    asc = b[::-1]
    pref = [0]*(N+1)
    s=0
    for i,val in enumerate(b):
        s += val
        pref[i+1] = s
    br = bisect_right
    Mval = M
    Rval = R
    Nval = N
    b_list = b
    pref_list = pref
    asc_list = asc
    rank_list = rank
    A_list = A
    ans = []
    for i in range(N):
        a = A_list[i]
        r = rank_list[i]
        # check hi
        T = a + Rval
        q = Nval - br(asc_list, T)
        # compute H at hi
        if r >= Mval:
            if q < Mval:
                cnt = Mval - q
                sum_b = pref_list[Mval] - pref_list[q]
            else:
                cnt = 0
                sum_b = 0
        else:
            if q < Mval + 1:
                cnt_base = Mval + 1 - q
                sum_base = pref_list[Mval+1] - pref_list[q]
            else:
                cnt_base = 0
                sum_base = 0
            if r >= q:
                cnt = cnt_base - 1
                sum_b = sum_base - b_list[r]
            else:
                cnt = cnt_base
                sum_b = sum_base
        H = cnt * (T + 1) - sum_b
        if H + Rval <= Rval? Wait hi x=R, condition H + x > R => H + R > R => H > 0. Actually if x=R, ok = H + R > R => H > 0. So can check H > 0. But in general use H + R > R. Since x=R. So if H <= 0 -> -1. But wait H can be 0 and x=R, condition false. So if H > 0 true else false. But if H >0. We can just if H <=0 ans=-1 continue. But note H at hi = sum deficits with T=a+R, S=0. If H>0 guaranteed. If H=0 impossible. Is it always H>=0 yes. So if H==0 impossible. But what if H>0 but condition H + R > R true. yes. So check hi by H > 0. But if R=0, hi=0, H>0 means ok(0). Good. We can use this to avoid binary search if impossible. But wait if H>0 at hi, ok true. If H=0, false. Since H nonnegative. Good.
        if H <= 0:
            ans.append("-1")
            continue
        # binary search lo=0 hi=R
        lo = 0
        hi = Rval
        # We know ok(hi) true. Need find min.
        while lo < hi:
            mid = (lo + hi) // 2
            T = a + mid
            q = Nval - br(asc_list, T)
            # compute H at mid
            if r >= Mval:
                if q < Mval:
                    cnt = Mval - q
                    sum_b = pref_list[Mval] - pref_list[q]
                else:
                    cnt = 0
                    sum_b = 0
            else:
                if q < Mval + 1:
                    cnt_base = Mval + 1 - q
                    sum_base = pref_list[Mval+1] - pref_list[q]
                else:
                    cnt_base = 0
                    sum_base = 0
                if r >= q:
                    cnt = cnt_base - 1
                    sum_b = sum_base - b_list[r]
                else:
                    cnt = cnt_base
                    sum_b = sum_base
            H = cnt * (T + 1) - sum_b
            if H + mid > Rval:
                hi = mid
            else:
                lo = mid + 1
        ans.append(str(lo))
    print(' '.join(ans))

But note in hi check, if Rval maybe 0, hi=0, H>0. If H<=0, ans -1. If H>0, binary search while lo<hi false, ans0. Good.

def solve():
    ...
    if M == N:
        print(" ".join(["0"]*N)); return
    ...
    prefM = pref[M]
    prefM1 = pref[M+1]
    br = bisect_right
    for i in range(N):
        a = A[i]; r = rank[i]
        # hi check
        T = a + R
        q = N - br(asc, T)
        if r >= M:
            if q < M:
                cnt = M - q
                sum_b = prefM - pref[q]
            else:
                cnt = 0; sum_b = 0
        else:
            if q < M1:
                cnt = M1 - q
                sum_b = prefM1 - pref[q]
            else:
                cnt = 0; sum_b = 0
            if r >= q:
                cnt -= 1
                sum_b -= b[r]
        H = cnt * (T + 1) - sum_b
        if H <= 0:
            ans.append("-1")
            continue
        lo=0; hi=R
        while lo < hi:
            mid = (lo+hi)//2
            T = a + mid
            q = N - br(asc, T)
            if r >= M:
                if q < M:
                    cnt = M - q
                    sum_b = prefM - pref[q]
                else:
                    cnt = 0; sum_b = 0
            else:
                if q < M1:
                    cnt = M1 - q
                    sum_b = prefM1 - pref[q]
                else:
                    cnt = 0; sum_b = 0
                if r >= q:
                    cnt -= 1
                    sum_b -= b[r]
            H = cnt * (T + 1) - sum_b
            if H + mid > R:
                hi = mid
            else:
                lo = mid + 1
        ans.append(str(lo))

Pseudo:

def solve():
    import sys
    from bisect import bisect_right
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N, M, K = data[:3]
    A = data[3:]
    R = K - sum(A)
    if M == N:
        sys.stdout.write(" ".join(["0"] * N))
        return
    order = sorted(range(N), key=A.__getitem__, reverse=True)
    b = [0]*N
    rank = [0]*N
    for pos, idx in enumerate(order):
        b[pos] = A[idx]
        rank[idx] = pos
    asc = b[::-1]
    pref = [0]*(N+1)
    s=0
    for i, val in enumerate(b):
        s += val
        pref[i+1] = s
    prefM = pref[M]
    prefM1 = pref[M+1]
    M1 = M + 1
    br = bisect_right
    ans = []
    for i in range(N):
        a = A[i]
        r = rank[i]
        # hi check
        T = a + R
        q = N - br(asc, T)
        if r >= M:
            if q < M:
                cnt = M - q
                sum_b = prefM - pref[q]
            else:
                cnt = 0
                sum_b = 0
        else:
            if q < M1:
                cnt = M1 - q
                sum_b = prefM1 - pref[q]
            else:
                cnt = 0
                sum_b = 0
            if r >= q:
                cnt -= 1
                sum_b -= b[r]
        H = cnt * (T + 1) - sum_b
        if H <= 0:
            ans.append("-1")
            continue
        lo = 0
        hi = R
        while lo < hi:
            mid = (lo + hi) // 2
            T = a + mid
            q = N - br(asc, T)
            if r >= M:
                if q < M:
                    cnt = M - q
                    sum_b = prefM - pref[q]
                else:
                    cnt = 0
                    sum_b = 0
            else:
                if q < M1:
                    cnt = M1 - q
                    sum_b = prefM1 - pref[q]
                else:
                    cnt = 0
                    sum_b = 0
                if r >= q:
                    cnt -= 1
                    sum_b -= b[r]
            H = cnt * (T + 1) - sum_b
            if H + mid > R:
                hi = mid
            else:
                lo = mid + 1
        ans.append(str(lo))
    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(ans))

Let's write final code fully:

import sys
from bisect import bisect_right

def solve():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N, M, K = data[0], data[1], data[2]
    A = data[3:3+N]
    R = K - sum(A)

    if M == N:
        sys.stdout.write(" ".join(["0"] * N) + "\n")
        return

    order = sorted(range(N), key=A.__getitem__, reverse=True)
    b = [0] * N
    rank = [0] * N
    for pos, idx in enumerate(order):
        b[pos] = A[idx]
        rank[idx] = pos

    asc = b[::-1]

    pref = [0] * (N + 1)
    s = 0
    for i, v in enumerate(b):
        s += v
        pref[i + 1] = s

    prefM = pref[M]
    prefM1 = pref[M + 1]
    M1 = M + 1
    br = bisect_right
    ans = []

    for i in range(N):
        a = A[i]
        r = rank[i]

        # Check x = R
        T = a + R
        q = N - br(asc, T)

        if r >= M:
            if q < M:
                cnt = M - q
                sum_b = prefM - pref[q]
            else:
                cnt = 0
                sum_b = 0
        else:
            if q < M1:
                cnt = M1 - q
                sum_b = prefM1 - pref[q]
            else:
                cnt = 0
                sum_b = 0
            if r >= q:
                cnt -= 1
                sum_b -= b[r]

        H = cnt * (T + 1) - sum_b

        if H <= 0:
            ans.append("-1")
            continue

        lo = 0
        hi = R
        while lo < hi:
            mid = (lo + hi) // 2
            T = a + mid
            q = N - br(asc, T)

            if r >= M:
                if q < M:
                    cnt = M - q
                    sum_b = prefM - pref[q]
                else:
                    cnt = 0
                    sum_b = 0
            else:
                if q < M1:
                    cnt = M1 - q
                    sum_b = prefM1 - pref[q]
                else:
                    cnt = 0
                    sum_b = 0
                if r >= q:
                    cnt -= 1
                    sum_b -= b[r]

            H = cnt * (T + 1) - sum_b

            if H + mid > R:
                hi = mid
            else:
                lo = mid + 1

        ans.append(str(lo))

    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(ans) + "\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    solve()
